- Maintain a normalized sum=1.
- Compute conv with FFT, max, weights (logsumexp).

FFT convolution optimized:
```
def conv_full(a):
    n=len(a)
    size=1
    while size < 2*n-1: size <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
    return c[:2*n-1]
```
For repeated calls, size may be fixed; we can cache next pow2? Top-level no closures but can use attributes. Simpler function.

Projection onto simplex {x≥0, sum=1}:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)+1) > css)[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
This sorts O(n log n). For n up to 1000 and 100k iterations, might be OK? 100k*1000log ~1e9 too high. Use faster EUCLID? We can use clip renormalize with occasional projection. Or implement O(n) projection via `np.partition`? For each iteration, sorting 500 maybe ~0.05ms? 100k=5s? Actually numpy sort 500 ~10us? 100k=1s, okay. For 2000 ~20us, 2s. Might be okay within 1500s if iterations not 100k. We can use projection.

But line search with multiple projections per iteration expensive. We can accept one projection and no line search? Use fixed step and track best. To avoid worsening, we can evaluate objective after each update (FFT) anyway. If worsen, reduce step, or use Barzilai? We can use backtracking: try step, if not improve exact obj, halve up to few times. Each trial requires convolution (FFT). Could be costly but okay for n≤500. We can instead use smooth logsumexp objective (same as conv already computed) and accept if L_τ decreases (not exact max). At end exact. But for high precision, line search exact beneficial.

Compute weights:
```
def soft_weights(c, tau_param, n):
    if tau_param <= 0:
        w=np.zeros(len(c)); w[np.argmax(c)]=1; return w
    tau=tau_param/n
    shifted=c/tau - max(c)/tau
    # if tau very small, may underflow, ok
    w=np.exp(np.clip(shifted,-700,0))
    s=w.sum()
    if s==0: ...
    return w/s
```
Since max shifted 0, exp≤1. For tau small, only max. Good.

Pseudo:
```
def local_optimize(a, budget, seed, step0=0.7, tau0=3.0, tau1=0.05, top_cycle=(1,3,10,30,100), use_lp=False):
    n=len(a); a=normalize(a)
    conv=conv_fft(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score(a,conv)
    step=step0; fail=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time-left>0.1:
       frac=(time.monotonic()-start)/budget
       tau=tau0+(tau1-tau0)*min(1,frac*2) # anneal first half
       # choose weights: if it % 5 ==4 topk else softmax? Let's do softmax primarily, with small tau.
       if use_topk and (it % 3 == 0):
           K=top_cycle[(it//3)%len(top_cycle)]
           w=top_weights(conv,K)
       else:
           w=soft_weights(conv,tau,n)
       p=grad(w,a)
       g=p - np.mean(p)
       # if norm too small break
       gn=np.linalg.norm(g)
       if gn < 1e-12: break
       # adaptive step scale: maybe normalize direction and use step/n? Since g scale 1/n but step0 O(1). We can not normalize.
       # But if g has large endpoint, step can be too big. Use line search.
       accepted=False
       trials=[step, step*0.5, step*0.25, step*0.125]
       for trial in trials:
           a_new=proj_simplex(a - trial*g)
           conv_new=conv_fft(a_new)
           s_new=score(a,conv_new) # n known
           if s_new < best_score - 1e-15:
               accept; break
           if s_new < current_score? maybe accept if best? If no improve best, maybe accept if smooth? We want monotonic best. But local can temporarily worsen to escape. Maybe allow small worsen.
       if accepted:
          if s_new < best_score: best...
          step = min(2.0, trial*1.1)
          fail=0
       else:
          step*=0.7
          fail+=1
          if fail>50: break or perturb
       a=a_new if accepted else a
       it+=1
```

def clip_norm(v):
   v=np.maximum(v,0)
   s=v.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   return v/s
```

Implement `proj_simplex` efficiently:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
    # assumes v 1d
    n=v.size
    u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css=np.cumsum(u)
    rho=np.nonzero(u*(np.arange(1,n+1)+1) > css)[0][-1]
    theta=(css[rho]-1)/(rho+1)
    out=v-theta
    out[out<0]=0
    s=out.sum()
    if s>0: out/=s  # ensure exact due numeric
    else: out=np.ones(n)/n
    return out
```
This has `np.nonzero` each iter. Could optimize by using `np.flatnonzero`. Fine.

But for line search with trial steps, we project each. Could instead compute penalty and use `np.maximum` faster. Let's maybe use `proj_simplex` only when step large? We'll start with true.

```
def build_A(B):
    n=len(B)
    k=np.arange(2*n-1)[:,None]
    j=np.arange(n)[None,:]
    idx=k-j
    # idx range -(n-1)..2n-2. pad B with zeros at front n-1 and back n-1?
    Bpad=np.concatenate([np.full(n-1,0.0), B, np.full(n-1,0.0)])
    A=Bpad[idx + (n-1)]  # because idx min -(n-1)
    # values where idx<0 or idx>=n should be zero; pad zeros there if Bpad includes zeros in those ranges. idx+(n-1) min0 max 4n-3? Bpad length 3n-1? idx max 2n-2, +n-1=3n-3, Bpad length 3n-1? ok.
    return A
```

Let's design local optimizer more concretely.

```
def local_subgrad(a, budget, seed=0, step0=0.8, tau0=8.0, tau1=0.05, allow_smooth=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a=normalize(a)
    conv=conv_fft(a)
    best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score(a,conv); cur_score=best_score
    step=step0; fail=0; it=0
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    beta=0.7
    # Loop
    while time.monotonic()-start < budget:
       frac=min(1.0,(time.monotonic()-start)/max(budget,1e-6))
       tau=tau0*(1-frac)+tau1*frac
       # weights choice: softmax mostly, topk every 4? Let's use softmax with tau and if tau<0.5 topk? 
       if it % 4 == 0:
           # topk cycle
           K = [1,3,10,30,100,200][(it//4) % 6]
           w = top_weights(conv, K)
       else:
           w = soft_weights(conv, tau, n)
       p = grad_weights(w,a)
       g = p - p.mean()
       # momentum
       momentum = beta*momentum + (1-beta)*g
       direction = momentum - momentum.mean()
       norm=np.linalg.norm(direction)
       if norm < 1e-13:
           fail+=1; step*=0.8; ...
       # line search
       accepted=False
       # If step too large, try decreasing; if previous good, try increasing occasionally
       trials = []
       base=step
       if it%10==0: trials=[base*1.5, base]
       else: trials=[base, base*0.5, base*0.25]
       for tri in trials:
           x=a - tri*direction
           x=proj_simplex(x)
           cn=conv_fft(x)
           sn=score(x,cn)
           if sn < cur_score - 1e-14:
                # optionally smooth check? accept
                accepted=True; a=x; conv=cn; cur_score=sn; break
           elif sn < best_score and not accepted? we want maybe accept if best? Use cur for monotone current. If no cur improve, maybe accept smooth if allow.
       if accepted:
           if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
               best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
           step = min(5.0, step*1.05)
           fail=0
       else:
           step *= 0.7
           fail +=1
           if fail > 100:
               # perturb best and continue
               a = perturb(best_a, rng, scale=0.1)
               conv=conv_fft(a); cur_score=score(a,conv)
               step=step0*0.5; fail=0
               momentum=zeros
       it+=1
    return best_a, best_score
```
This uses exact current_score monotone. It may stall. Add smooth acceptance: If no exact current improvement but smooth L improves and exact score < best_score*1.05? Could accept to navigate. Let's implement optional: compute `L_cur` from conv,tau; for trial, compute `L_new` same tau. If `sn < cur_score + 1e-8` maybe accept if L_new < L_cur - 1e-8? But cur_score would increase, may lead away. Maybe better to keep a "current" that can vary, and best separate. Use simulated annealing temperature:
- Start temp = 0.001? Accept if sn < cur_score + temp. Update cur_score. If best not improve for many, perturb.
This allows escapes. Temperature decrease with iterations.

Let's implement acceptance:
```
temp = 1e-5 * (1 - frac) + 1e-8? Actually score around1.5; allow 1e-6.
if sn < cur_score + temp: accept
else reject.
```
But exact objective non-smooth; temp 1e-5 fine. If smooth L improves, accept even if sn < cur_score+0.001? Maybe.

Simplify: Accept if `sn < cur_score + 1e-8`. Monotone. Perturb on stall. Good.

- `a = best_a * exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)); normalize` with sig increasing (0.1,0.3,0.6)
- `a = mix(best_a, random_init, t=0.5)`

`top_weights(c, K)`:
```
m=len(c)
if K<=0: K=1
if K>=m: w=np.full(m,1/m); return
# use argpartition for top K, but if K=1 use argmax
if K==1:
   idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w=zero; w[idx]=1
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(c, m-K)[-K:]  # top K unsorted
   # weight proportional to c? Or uniform? For max subgradient, any convex combo of active if all equal; for near active, proportional to c maybe better.
   vals=c[idx]
   widx=vals/vals.sum()  # emphasizes larger
   w=zero; w[idx]=widx
```
Proportional to c among top may help. But if values vary, top1 still. Could use uniform top K to focus plateau. We can have a parameter `prop_top` True.

`soft_weights(c, tau_param, n)`:
```
tau=tau_param/n
if tau<=0: top1
shift=(c-cmax)/tau
# To avoid underflow, clip min -745
w=np.exp(np.maximum(shift,-745))
# If all underflow, top1
s=w.sum()
if s==0: top1
return w/s
```
If tau large, shift small negative, weights not too uniform? If tau=10/n, cmax-cmin~1/n, shift ~-0.1, okay. If tau=100/n, shift ~-0.01, very broad. Good.

Maybe use temperature in log-sum-exp objective `L = cmax + tau*logsumexp((c-cmax)/tau)`. If tau broad, gradient broad.

But if direction from top1 has p with 1000 nonzeros? Norm maybe √n*(1/n)=1/√n. Good.

Let's implement `normalize(a)` robust:
```
def normalize(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-12: return np.ones(len(a))/len(a)
    return a/s
```

`score(a, conv=None)`:
```
n=len(a)
if conv is None: conv=conv_fft(a)
S=a.sum()
if S < 0.01: return np.inf (but our normalized S=1)
return 2.0*n*np.max(conv)/ (S*S)
```
Since normalized S=1, `2*n*max`.

```
_FFT_CACHE = {}
def fft_size(n):
   need=2*n-1
   size=1
   while size<need: size<<=1
   return size
```
In local loop, size constant. We can implement `conv_fft_fixed(a, size)` to avoid while. But top-level helper:
```
def conv_fft(a, size=None):
   n=len(a)
   if size is None: size=...
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,size)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,size)
   return c[:2*n-1]
```
In loop call with size.

x = a - trial*direction
if np.all(x >= -1e-12):
    x = np.maximum(x,0)
    s=x.sum(); if s>1e-12: x/=s
else:
    x = proj_simplex(x)
```
This avoids sort when positive. Good.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb(a, rng, scale=0.1):
   a2 = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0, scale, len(a)))
   a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0)
   return normalize(a2)
```

```
def perturb_add(a,rng,scale=0.01):
   a2=a+rng.normal(0,scale,len(a)); normalize
```

Now, LP polish implementation details:
```
def lp_polish(a, budget, seed=0, t_values=(0.5,0.25,0.1), max_iter=None):
   start=time.monotonic()
   n=len(a); a=normalize(a)
   conv=conv_fft(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score(a,conv)
   for it in range(...):
      if time. budget exceeded break
      M_a = np.max(conv)
      B = n*a
      M_b = n*M_a
      A = build_A_dense(B) # vectorized
      cvec = -np.ones(n)
      bvec = np.full(2*n-1, M_b)
      bounds = [(0.0,None)]*n
      try:
         res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True,'time_limit':5})
         if res.status not in (0,3) or res.x is None: break
         g=res.x
         S=g.sum()
         if S <= 1+1e-6: break
         g = g/S
         accepted=False
         for t in t_values:
            a_new=(1-t)*a + t*g
            a_new=normalize(np.maximum(a_new,0))
            cn=conv_fft(a_new); sn=score(a_new,cn)
            if sn < best_score - 1e-12:
                accept; break
         if accept:
            a=a_new; conv=cn; best...
         else:
            # maybe try t=0.05,0.01
            ... if not break
      except: break
   return best_a,best_score
```

```
def grad_weights(w,a,sparse_idx=None):
   n=len(a)
   if sparse_idx is not None and len(sparse_idx)<=64:
       p=np.zeros(n)
       for k in sparse_idx:
          wk=w[k]
          if wk==0: continue
          i_min=max(0,k-n+1); i_max=min(n-1,k)
          if i_min<=i_max:
             seg_len=i_max-i_min+1
             j_start=k-i_max
             p[j_start:j_start+seg_len] += (2.0*wk)*a[i_min:i_min+seg_len][::-1]
       return p
   # if n<=250 use correlate? But direct correlate with dense w:
   if n <= 250:
       return 2.0*np.correlate(w,a,mode='valid')
   # FFT
```

Now, family inits should be normalized. Let's implement generic:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a=np.ones_like(a)
        s=a.sum()
    return a/s
```

`power_seq(n, alpha, side)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)+0.5
if side==0: base=x
elif side==1: base=n-x
else: base=np.sqrt(x*(n-x))
return normalize(base**(-alpha))
```
If alpha=0, uniform.

`two_side_seq(n, alpha, beta)`:
```
x=np.arange(n)+0.5; y=n-x; return normalize(x**(-alpha)*y**(-beta))
```

`exp_seq(n, lam, side)`:
```
x=np.arange(n); if side0: base=np.exp(-lam*x); else base=np.exp(-lam*(n-1-x)); # add small floor? normalize
```
Exponential decays at other end zero maybe; okay.

`random_cos_seq(n, rng)`:
```
x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
a=1.0
for freq in rng.integers(1,5, size=rng.integers(1,3)):
   amp=rng.uniform(0.2,1.0)
   phase=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
   a += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*freq*x+phase)
a=np.maximum(a,0.05)
return normalize(a)
```
Maybe too random.

`resample_seq(a, n)`:
```
m=len(a)
if m==n: return normalize(a)
if m==1: return np.full(n, a[0])
xo=np.linspace(0,1,m)
xn=np.linspace(0,1,n)
new=np.interp(xn,xo,a)
return normalize(np.maximum(new,0))
```

`make_inits(n, seed, parent=None, max_inits=20)`:
- Create list of seqs.
- If parent not None, resample parent to n.
- Add families.
- Evaluate scores; sort; return top K with scores.
Need top-level function. It will call `score` (FFT). For many n, cost okay.
```
def make_inits(n, seed, parent=None, k=12):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    seqs=[]
    if parent is not None and parent.size>0:
       seqs.append(resample_seq(parent,n))
    # uniform
    seqs.append(np.ones(n))
    for alpha in [0.0,0.2,0.35,0.45,0.49]:
       seqs.append(power_seq(n,alpha,0))
       seqs.append(power_seq(n,alpha,1))
    for alpha,beta in [(0.49,0.05),(0.45,0.2),(0.3,0.3),(0.05,0.49),(0.25,0.45),(0.45,0.45)]:
       seqs.append(two_side_seq(n,alpha,beta))
    for lam in [0.3,0.8,1.7,3.5]:
       seqs.append(exp_seq(n,lam,0))
       seqs.append(exp_seq(n,lam,1))
    for r in range(4):
       seqs.append(random_cos_seq(n,rng))
    # evaluate
    scored=[]
    for s in seqs:
       sn=score(s)
       if np.isfinite(sn): scored.append((sn,s))
    scored.sort(key=lambda x:x[0])  # lambda not allowed? Rule no lambda functions! We must avoid lambda. Use `scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))` from operator? Or implement simple selection. We can import operator.itemgetter.
```
No lambda functions. Use `from operator import itemgetter` top-level, then `scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))`. Good.

Let's write local_subgrad in detail with no nested functions:
```
def local_subgrad(a, budget, seed=0, tau0=10.0, tau1=0.05, step0=0.6, max_step=3.0, top_frac=0.25, top_cycle=(3,5,10,20,50,100), verbose=False):
    start=time.monotonic()
    n=len(a)
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    conv=conv_fft(a)
    best_a=a.copy()
    best_score=score_seq(a, conv)
    cur_score=best_score
    cur_a=a.copy()
    momentum=np.zeros(n)
    step=step0
    fail=0
    it=0
    top_period=max(1,int(round(1.0/top_frac)))
    fft_grad_size=1
    while fft_grad_size < 3*n: fft_grad_size <<=1
    while time.monotonic()-start < budget:
        elapsed=time.monotonic()-start
        frac=elapsed/budget if budget>0 else 1.0
        if frac > 1: frac=1.0
        tau=tau0 + (tau1-tau0)*frac
        if it % top_period == 0:
           K=top_cycle[(it//top_period) % len(top_cycle)]
           w, idx = top_weights(conv, K)
        else:
           w, idx = soft_weights(conv, tau, n)
        p=grad_weights(w,a,idx)
        g=p - p.mean()
        momentum = 0.4*momentum + 0.6*g
        direction = momentum - momentum.mean()
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))
        if dnorm < 1e-12:
            fail += 1
            if fail > 50: perturb ...
            else:
                # random tiny kick
                a = cur_a? maybe break
            continue
        # line search
        accepted=False
        # trial selection: start with step, maybe try larger if success count
        trials=[]
        if step < 0.1: trials=[step*2]
        else:
            if it % 5 == 0 and step < max_step:
                trials.append(min(max_step, step*1.5))
            trials.append(step)
            trials.append(step*0.5)
            trials.append(step*0.25)
        for tri in trials:
            if tri <= 0: continue
            x = a - tri*direction
            x = project_to_simplex(x)
            cn = conv_fft(x)
            sn = score_seq(x, cn)
            if sn < cur_score - 1e-14:
                a=x; conv=cn; cur_score=sn; accepted=True; step=tri
                break
            # if smooth? maybe compute if not
        if accepted:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-14:
                best_score=cur_score; best_a=a.copy()
            if step < max_step: step = min(max_step, step*1.15)
            fail=0
        else:
            step *= 0.6
            fail +=1
            if fail > 80:
                # perturb best
                scale = 0.05 + 0.05*((fail//80)%4)
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, scale)
                conv = conv_fft(a)
                cur_score = score_seq(a, conv)
                step = max(0.1, step0*0.5)
                momentum = np.zeros(n)
                fail = 0
        it +=1
        if verbose and it % 1000 ==0: print(...)
    return best_a,best_score
```
But after reject, we keep a old. Good.
`tri` set in loop; if not accepted, `tri` undefined? Python loop variable defined if trials nonempty. Fine.

Maybe accept if `sn < cur_score + 1e-12` to allow numerical? Strict improvement. If no exact improvement but smooth, may stall. Could implement temperature:
```
temp = 1e-8 + 1e-4*(1-frac)
if sn < cur_score + temp: accept with warn?```

def project_to_simplex(v):
    n=v.size
    # quick clipping
    x=np.maximum(v,0.0)
    s=x.sum()
    if s>0:
        x=x/s

```
def project_to_simplex(v):
    n=len(v)
    s=float(v.sum())
    if s <= 0: return np.ones(n)/n
    if np.min(v) >= -1e-12 and abs(s-1) < 1e-8:
        out=np.maximum(v,0); return out/out.sum()
    return proj_simplex(v)
```
Good.

Perturb function:
```
def perturb_seq(a, rng, scale):
    n=len(a)
    mode=rng.integers(0,3)
    if mode==0:
       a2=a*np.exp(rng.normal(0,scale,n))
    elif mode==1:
       a2=a + rng.normal(0, scale*0.1,n)
    else:
       # mix with smooth random
       a2 = a * (1-scale) + random_cos_seq(n, rng)? Can't call random_cos with rng? It expects rng, okay.
    return normalize_seq(a2)
```
No nested. `random_cos_seq` top-level.

But `perturb_seq` mode 2 needs n; okay.

Now, `lp_polish` top-level. Build A dense vectorized. Need import `linprog`. If scipy not available? It is allowed.

```
def build_A_dense(B):
    n=len(B)
    k=np.arange(2*n-1)[:,None]
    j=np.arange(n)[None,:]
    idx=k-j
    # pad B to length 4*n? idx range -n+1 to 2n-2. offset n-1 => 0..3n-3.
    pad_len=4*n
    Bpad=np.zeros(pad_len, dtype=float)
    Bpad[n-1:n-1+n]=B
    return Bpad[idx+n-1]
```
This creates idx int array (2n-1,n) and Bpad float. For n=800, int array ~1M*8=8MB, float 10MB, okay. For n=1500, int 18MB, float 18MB? Actually 2n*n=4.5M, int 36MB, float 36MB; maybe ok but LP skip. We'll limit.
Alternative use `np.subtract.outer` same.

`lp_polish` should guard if `n>800` return unchanged.
```
def lp_polish(a, budget, seed=0, max_lp_time=5.0):
    start=time.monotonic(); n=len(a)
    if n > 800: return normalize_seq(a), score...
    a=normalize_seq(a)
    conv=conv_fft(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_score=score_seq(a,conv)
    it=0
    while time.monotonic()-start < budget:
       M_a=float(np.max(conv))
       B=n*a
       M_b=n*M_a
       A=build_A_dense(B)
       cvec=-np.ones(n)
       bvec=np.full(2*n-1,M_b)
       bounds=[(0.0,None)] * n
       try:
          res=linprog(cvec, A_ub=A, b_ub=bvec, bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve':True, 'time_limit':max_lp_time})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.status not in (0,3) or getattr(res,'x',None) is None:
          if res.status==3 and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None: use? Maybe skip.
          break
       g=res.x
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): break
       S=float(np.sum(g))
       if S <= 1.0 + 1e-6:
          # no primal improvement; break or continue with smaller? break
          break
       g=normalize_seq(g)
       accepted=False
       for t in (0.5,0.25,0.1,0.05):
           a_new=(1.0-t)*a + t*g
           a_new=normalize_seq(a_new)
           cn=conv_fft(a_new)
           sn=score_seq(a_new,cn)
           if sn < best_score - 1e-12:
              a=a_new; conv=cn; best_a=a_new.copy(); best_score=sn; accepted=True; break
       if not accepted:
          # try very small t
          found=False
          for t in (0.02,0.01,0.005): ...
          if not found: break
       it+=1
    return best_a,best_score
```
If LP returns same a (S close 1), local optimum for LP; break.

Need be careful: `linprog` with infeasible? Feasible. `bvec` positive. If `M_a` tiny? normalized, okay.

def optimize_n(n, inits, budget, seed, verbose=True):
    # inits sorted list of (score, seq)
    start=time.monotonic()
    best_a=None; best_score=np.inf
    # allocate time: give more to first (better init), but allow each at least min
    # If many inits, we can run first with 60% then others? Maybe.
    # Simpler: for each init index, remaining = ...; alloc = min(remaining/ (len-in), max(5, budget*0.25))
    for i,(init_score,seq) in enumerate(inits):
       remaining = deadline? We can inspect time.
       if time.monotonic()-start >= budget: break
       rem = budget - (time.monotonic()-start)
       if i == 0:
          alloc = min(rem*0.5, max(20, budget*0.4))
       else:
          alloc = min(rem/(len(inits)-i), max(5, budget*0.15))
       a,s = local_subgrad(seq, alloc, seed=seed+i*1000, verbose=verbose)
       if s < best_score: best_score=s; best_a=a.copy()
       # if time low break
    return best_a,best_score
```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    start=time.monotonic(); hard_start=start
    deadline=start+float(budget_s)-20.0
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    # parent
    parent=None
    try:
       par=np.asarray(height_sequence_1,dtype=float)
       par=par[np.isfinite(par)]
       if par.size>0:
          par=np.clip(par,0,1000)
          if par.sum()>0:
             parent=normalize_seq(par)
             parent_score=score_seq(parent)
             if parent_score < best_score: best_score=parent_score; best_seq=parent.copy()
    except Exception:
       parent=None
    # candidate lengths
    n_set=set([200,250,300,400,500,700,1000,1500,2000])

       lp=parent.size

       n_set.add(max(50,int(lp)-50))

Can we speed LP by reducing constraints to active peaks? Full LP has 2n-1 constraints. But many constraints are not binding. Active set size maybe <n. We can solve a *working set LP* with top K constraints (active peaks of a*a) plus maybe previous binding. This can be much faster and perhaps as good. Let's design `lp_active(a, K=200)`.

Given a, M=max c. Choose top K indices where c near max (or all c > M - tol). Solve max sum g s.t. (a*g)_k ≤ M for k in active set, g≥0. Fewer constraints (K). This is an LP with A_active K×n. If K=200,n=1000 =>200k entries, faster. The solution may violate other constraints, but mixing with small t maybe safe. We can evaluate exact and accept if improves. If violates too much, objective may worsen; line search small t. We can use active LP instead of full for larger n. This could be faster and allow n up to 2000 with K=500.

Let's refine `soft_weights` to handle tau_param. If tau_param<=1e-8: top 1 or top small. For top, use top_weights(c, 1) but returns sparse idx.
```
def soft_weights(c,tau_param,n):
   if tau_param <= 1e-8:
      return top_weights(c,1)
   tau=tau_param/float(n)
   cmax=float(np.max(c))
   shifted=(c-cmax)/tau
   # If shifted min too negative, clip. Since max 0, negative.
   shifted=np.maximum(shifted, -745.0)
   w=np.exp(shifted)
   s=w.sum()
   if s < 1e-300:
      return top_weights(c,1)
   w=w/s
   return w,None
```
For tau large, shifted near 0, w sum large but normalized.

`top_weights` for K maybe > len(c), return uniform. If K small, idx. For proportional weights, if vals sum 0, uniform.
```
def top_weights(c,K):
   m=len(c)
   if K<=0: K=1
   if K>=m:
      w=np.full(m,1.0/m); return w,None
   if K==1:
      idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
      w=np.zeros(m); w[idx[0]]=1.0; return w,idx
   part=np.argpartition(c, m-K)
   idx=part[m-K:]
   vals=c[idx]
   s=vals.sum()
   if s <=0: vals=np.ones(K)
   weights=vals/vals.sum()
   w=np.zeros(m)
   w[idx]=weights
   return w,idx
```
`argpartition` with m-K if K=m? handled. If K large e.g. 100, okay. For K small, fast. For K large (e.g., 500), argpartition O(m). Good.

def get_grad_size(n):
    val=_FFT_GRAD_SIZE.get(n)
    if val is None:
       size=1
       need=3*n  # 3n-2 but 3n safe
       while size<need: size<<=1
       _FFT_GRAD_SIZE[n]=size
       val=size
    return val
```

`score_seq`:
```
def score_seq(a, conv=None):
    n=len(a)
    if n<=0: return np.inf
    if conv is None: conv=conv_fft(a)
    s=float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01: return np.inf
    mx=float(np.max(conv))
    return 2.0*float(n)*mx/(s*s)
```
Our a normalized sum1. For family, score_seq uses sum.

cond = u*(np.arange(1,n+1)+1) > css
rho = np.flatnonzero(cond)
if len(rho)==0: theta = (css[-1]-1)/n? else rho[-1]
```
Standard.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+float(budget_s)-20.0
    best_seq=None
    best_score=np.inf

    parent=None
    try:
       par=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=float)
       par=par[np.isfinite(par)]
       if par.size>0:
          par=np.clip(par,0.0,1000.0)
          if par.sum()>0.0:
             parent=normalize_seq(par)
    except Exception:
       parent=None

    if parent is not None:
       sc=score_seq(parent)
       if sc < best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_seq=parent.copy()
       print("parent n", parent.size, sc)

    n_set=set([200,250,300,400,500,700,1000,1500,2000])
    if parent is not None:
       lp=parent.size
       n_set.add(int(lp))
       n_set.add(max(50, int(lp)-50))
       n_set.add(min(3000, int(lp)+50))
    n_values=[]
    for n in n_set:
       n=int(n)
       if 50 <= n <= 3000: n_values.append(n)
    n_values.sort()

    init_info={}
    quick_order=[]
    for n in n_values:
       inits=make_inits(n, seed+n*101, parent=parent, k=8)
       init_info[n]=inits
       if inits:
          sc,in_seq=inits[0]
          quick_order.append((sc,n))
          if sc < best_score:
             best_score=sc; best_seq=in_seq.copy()
    quick_order.sort(key=itemgetter(0,1))

    # n per best score dict
    n_best={}
    if best_seq is not None:
       n_best[len(best_seq)]=(best_score,best_seq)
    # also from quick inits? best_seq maybe.
```

```
def build_A_active(B, active_idx):
    n=len(B)
    active=np.asarray(active_idx, dtype=int)
    cols=np.arange(n)[None,:]
    idx=active[:,None]-cols
    Bpad=np.zeros(4*n, dtype=float)
    Bpad[n-1:n-1+n]=B
    return Bpad[idx+n-1]
```

Let's define:
```
_LBFGS_STATE = {}
def lbfgs_objective(u, state):
    # state has tau, n
    a=np.exp(u - np.max(u)); a/=a.sum()
    conv=conv_fft(a, state['size'])
    c=conv
    tau=state['tau']
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    shifted=(c-maxc)/tau; clipped
    L=maxc + tau*np.log(np.sum(np.exp(shifted)))
    # gradient
    w = softmax weights
    p = grad_weights(w,a,None,state['grad_size'])
    du = p - a*np.sum(p)
    return L, du
```

def _lbfgs_fun(u): return lbfgs_objective(u, _LBFGS_STATE)[0]
def _lbfgs_jac(u): return lbfgs_objective(u, _LBFGS_STATE)[1]
```

```python
"""
Search for nonnegative step-function heights minimizing
  2 * len(a) * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The optimizer uses:
- parameterized initializers (one-sided singular beta-like families, random chordal shapes, parent resampling),
- FFT-accelerated convolution and gradients,
- projected gradient descent for a log-sum-exp smoothed max-convolution objective,
- adaptive top-active/full-softmax weights, line search, and restarts,
- full LP polishing for moderate sequence lengths.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from operator import itemgetter
from scipy.optimize import linprog

_CONV_SIZE_CACHE = {}
_GRAD_SIZE_CACHE = {}


def get_conv_size(n):
    need = 2 * int(n) - 1
    size = 1
    while size < need:
        size <<= 1
    val = _CONV_SIZE_CACHE.get(n)
    if val is None:
        _CONV_SIZE_CACHE[n] = size
        return size
    return val


def get_grad_size(n):
    n = int(n)
    val = _GRAD_SIZE_CACHE.get(n)
    if val is None:
        need = 3 * n  # safely larger than 3n-2
        size = 1
        while size < need:
            size <<= 1
        _GRAD_SIZE_CACHE[n] = size
        return size
    return val


def conv_fft(a, size=None):
    """Full convolution a*a by FFT, linear part only."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if size is None:
        size = get_conv_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, size)
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def score_seq(a, conv=None):
    """Objective value for a sequence (scale invariant)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.inf
    if conv is None:
        conv = conv_fft(a)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    mx = float(np.max(np.real(conv)))
    return float(2.0 * n * mx / (s * s))


def normalize_seq(a):
    """Normalize to nonnegative sum=1 in [0,1000]."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 1e-12:
        a = np.ones_like(a, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(a.size)
    return a / s


def proj_simplex(v):
    """Euclidean projection onto {x >= 0, sum(x)=1}."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = v.size
    if n == 0:
        return v
    u = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    css = np.cumsum(u)
    idx = np.flatnonzero(u * (np.arange(1, n + 1) + 1.0) > css)
    if idx.size == 0:
        theta = (float(css[-1]) - 1.0) / float(n)
    else:
        rho = int(idx[-1])
        theta = (float(css[rho]) - 1.0) / float(rho + 1)
    out = v - theta
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    out /= s
    return out


def project_to_simplex(v):
    """Fast projection helper: exact simplex projection, with quick common case."""
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    s = float(v.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(v.size, 1.0 / v.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if np.min(v) >= -1e-12 and abs(s - 1.0) < 1e-7:
        out = np.maximum(v, 0.0)
        ss = float(out.sum())
        if ss > 1e-15:
            out /= ss
        else:
            out = np.full(v.size, 1.0 / v.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return out
    return proj_simplex(v)


def top_weights(c, K):
    """Weights on top K convolution coefficients, proportional to their values."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    m = len(c)
    K = int(K)
    if K <= 0:
        K = 1
    if K >= m:
        w = np.full(m, 1.0 / m, dtype=np.float64)
        return w, None
    if K == 1:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=int)
        w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx[0]] = 1.0
        return w, idx
    part = np.argpartition(c, m - K)
    idx = part[m - K:]
    vals = c[idx]
    s = float(vals.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        weights = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64) / float(K)
    else:
        weights = vals / s
    w = np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = weights
    return w, idx


def soft_weights(c, tau_param, n):
    """Log-sum-exp softmax weights over convolution coefficients."""
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    if tau_param <= 1e-12:
        return top_weights(c, 1)
    tau = float(tau_param) / float(max(1, int(n)))
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    shifted = (c - cmax) / tau
    shifted = np.maximum(shifted, -745.0)
    w = np.exp(shifted)
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s < 1e-300:
        return top_weights(c, 1)
    w /= s
    return w, None


def grad_weights(w, a, sparse_idx=None, size=None):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k * conv(a,a)_k with respect to a.
    Returns 2 * sum_i w_{i+j} a_i for j=0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(a)

    if sparse_idx is not None and len(sparse_idx) <= 64:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in sparse_idx:
            kk = int(kk)
            wk = float(w[kk])
            if wk == 0.0:
                continue
            i_min = max(0, kk - n + 1)
            i_max = min(n - 1, kk)
            if i_min > i_max:
                continue
            seg_len = i_max - i_min + 1
            j_start = kk - i_max
            p[j_start:j_start + seg_len] += (2.0 * wk) * a[i_min:i_min + seg_len][::-1]
        return p

    if n <= 260:
        return 2.0 * np.correlate(w, a, mode="valid")

    if size is None:
        size = get_grad_size(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, size)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, size)
    v = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(fa) * fw, size)[:n]
    return 2.0 * np.real(v)


def random_cos_seq(n, rng):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = int(rng.integers(1, 4, size=None))
    for _ in range(m):
        freq = int(rng.integers(1, 6, size=None))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 1.00))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        a += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * freq * x + phase)
    return normalize_seq(np.maximum(a, 0.02))


def power_seq(n, alpha, side):
    """One-sided singular family: (i+1/2)^-alpha or reversed."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    y = float(n) - x
    if side == 0:
        base = x
    else:
        base = y
    if alpha == 0.0:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        a = np.power(base, -float(alpha))
    return normalize_seq(a)


def two_side_seq(n, alpha, beta):
    """Two-sided singular family: (i+1/2)^-alpha * (n-i)^-beta."""
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5
    y = float(n) - x
    a = np.power(x, -float(alpha)) * np.power(y, -float(beta))
    return normalize_seq(a)


def exp_seq(n, lam, side):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if side == 0:
        a = np.exp(-float(lam) * x)
    else:
        a = np.exp(-float(lam) * (n - 1 - x))
    return normalize_seq(a)


def resample_seq(a, n):
    """Linearly resample a sequence/density to a new length."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    m = int(a.size)
    n = int(n)
    if m == n:
        return a.copy()
    if m == 1:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    return normalize_seq(np.interp(xn, xo, a))


def make_inits(n, seed, parent=None, k=8):
    """Create and score a variety of initializers for length n."""
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    seqs = []

    if parent is not None and parent.size > 0:
        seqs.append(resample_seq(parent, n))

    seqs.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    for alpha in (0.0, 0.20, 0.35, 0.45, 0.49):
        seqs.append(power_seq(n, alpha, 0))
        seqs.append(power_seq(n, alpha, 1))

    for alpha, beta in (
        (0.49, 0.05), (0.45, 0.20), (0.30, 0.30),
        (0.05, 0.49), (0.25, 0.45), (0.45, 0.45)
    ):
        seqs.append(two_side_seq(n, alpha, beta))

    for lam in (0.30, 0.80, 1.70, 3.50):
        seqs.append(exp_seq(n, lam, 0))
        seqs.append(exp_seq(n, lam, 1))

    base = power_seq(n, 0.49, 0)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    for amp in (0.20, 0.50):
        for mult in (1, 2, 3):
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            mod = base * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * float(mult) * x + phase))
            seqs.append(normalize_seq(np.maximum(mod, 0.0)))

    for _ in range(5):
        seqs.append(random_cos_seq(n, rng))

    scored = []
    for s in seqs:
        s = normalize_seq(s)
        sc = score_seq(s)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, s))

    if not scored:
        u = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
        return [(score_seq(u), u)]

    scored.sort(key=itemgetter(0))
    return scored[:max(1, int(k))]


def perturb_seq(a, rng, scale):
    """Localized perturbation for escape from local minima."""
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    mode = int(rng.integers(0, 3, size=None))
    scale = float(np.clip(scale, 0.01, 1.2))
    if mode == 0:
        b = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, scale, n))
    elif mode == 1:
        b = a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.10 * scale + 0.002, n)
    else:
        b = 0.75 * a + 0.25 * random_cos_seq(n, rng)
    return normalize_seq(np.maximum(b, 0.0))


def local_subgrad(a, budget, seed=0, tau0=10.0, tau1=0.05, step0=0.7,
                  max_step=3.0, top_frac=0.35,
                  top_cycle=(3, 5, 10, 20, 50, 100), verbose=False):
    """
    Projected gradient / line search for minimizing max convolution.
    Uses log-sum-exp smoothing and adaptive active-set weights.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or budget <= 0.0:
        return a, score_seq(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    size1 = get_conv_size(n)
    size2 = get_grad_size(n)

    conv = conv_fft(a, size1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_seq(a, conv)
    cur_score = best_score

    momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = float(step0)
    fail = 0
    it = 0

    top_period = max(1, int(round(1.0 / max(0.05, float(top_frac)))))
    cycle_len = len(top_cycle)

    while time.monotonic() - start < max(0.1, float(budget)) - 0.05:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
        frac = min(1.0, elapsed / max(1e-9, float(budget)))
        tau = float(tau0) + (float(tau1) - float(tau0)) * frac

        if it % top_period == 0:
            K = int(top_cycle[(it // top_period) % cycle_len])
            w, idx = top_weights(conv, K)
        else:
            w, idx = soft_weights(conv, tau, n)

        p = grad_weights(w, a, idx, size=size2)
        g = p - float(np.mean(p))
        momentum = 0.35 * momentum + 0.65 * g
        direction = momentum - float(np.mean(momentum))
        dnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(direction))

        if dnorm < 1e-13:
            fail += 5
            if fail > 100:
                scale = 0.05 + 0.08 * ((fail // 100) % 8)
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, scale)
                conv = conv_fft(a, size1)
                cur_score = score_seq(a, conv)
                step = max(0.15, float(step0) * 0.6)
                momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                fail = 0
            else:
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, 0.02)
                conv = conv_fft(a, size1)
                cur_score = score_seq(a, conv)
            it += 1
            continue

        trials = []
        if it % 5 == 0 and step < max_step:
            trials.append(min(float(max_step), step * 1.75))
        trials.append(step)
        trials.append(step * 0.5)
        trials.append(step * 0.25)

        accepted = False
        best_trial = None
        for tri in trials:
            if tri <= 1e-15:
                continue
            x = a - tri * direction
            x = project_to_simplex(x)
            cn = conv_fft(x, size1)
            sn = score_seq(x, cn)
            if sn < cur_score - 1e-12:
                a = x
                conv = cn
                cur_score = sn
                accepted = True
                best_trial = tri
                break

        if accepted:
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-13:
                best_score = cur_score
                best_a = a.copy()
            step = min(float(max_step), float(best_trial) * 1.12)
            fail = 0
        else:
            step *= 0.62
            fail += 1
            if fail > 120:
                scale = 0.05 + 0.08 * ((fail // 120) % 8)
                a = perturb_seq(best_a, rng, scale)
                conv = conv_fft(a, size1)
                cur_score = score_seq(a, conv)
                step = max(0.15, float(step0) * 0.55)
                momentum = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                fail = 0

        if verbose and it % 2000 == 0:
            print(f"  local n={n} it={it} cur={cur_score:.8f} best={best_score:.8f}")

        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def build_A_dense(B):
    """
    Dense matrix A for LP constraints (B*g)_k <= rhs.
    B has length n, A has shape (2n-1, n), A[k,j]=B[k-j] if valid.
    """
    B = np.asarray(B, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(B.size)
    k = np.arange(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
    idx = k - j
    Bpad = np.zeros(4 * n, dtype=np.float64)
    Bpad[n - 1:n - 1 + n] = B
    return Bpad[idx + (n - 1)]


def lp_polish(a, budget, seed=0, max_n=700):
    """
    Alternating LP polish.
    Solves: max sum(g) s.t. B*g <= n*max(conv(a,a)), 0 <= g <= 100,
    where B = n*a. Mixes g back into a.
    """
    a = normalize_seq(a)
    n = a.size
    if n <= 0 or budget <= 0.0 or n > int(max_n):
        return a, score_seq(a)

    start = time.monotonic()
    size1 = get_conv_size(n)
    conv = conv_fft(a, size1)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score_seq(a, conv)

    it = 0
    while time.monotonic() - start < max(0.1, float(budget)) - 0.2:
        M_a = float(np.max(conv))
        if M_a <= 0.0:
            break

        B = n * a
        M_b = n * M_a
        A = build_A_dense(B)
        cvec = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        bvec = np.full(2 * n - 1, M_b, dtype=np.float64)
        bounds = [(0.0, 100.0)] * n

        try:
            res = linprog(
                cvec,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=bvec,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": 6}
            )
        except Exception:
            break

        if getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0 or getattr(res, "x", None) is None:
            break

        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        Sg = float(np.sum(g))
        if Sg <= 1.0 + 1e-7:
            break

        g = normalize_seq(g)
        accepted = False
        for t in (0.45, 0.25, 0.12, 0.05, 0.02):
            a_new = (1.0 - t) * a + t * g
            a_new = normalize_seq(a_new)
            cn = conv_fft(a_new, size1)
            sn = score_seq(a_new, cn)
            if sn < best_score - 1e-12:
                a = a_new
                conv = cn
                best_a = a_new.copy()
                best_score = sn
                accepted = True
                break

        if not accepted:
            break

        if it % 3 == 0:
            print(f"  LP n={n} it={it} score={best_score:.8f} sumg={Sg:.4f}")
        it += 1

    return best_a, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500):
    """
    Top-level search routine.
    Returns a Python list of nonnegative heights.
    """
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + float(budget_s) - 20.0

    best_seq = None
    best_score = np.inf
    n_best = {}

    parent = None
    try:
        par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        par = par[np.isfinite(par)]
        if par.size > 0:
            par = np.clip(par, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if float(par.sum()) > 0.0:
                parent = normalize_seq(par)
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        sc = score_seq(parent)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = parent.copy()
        n_best[int(parent.size)] = (sc, parent.copy())
        print(f"parent n={parent.size} score={sc:.8f}")

    n_set = set([200, 250, 300, 400, 500, 700, 1000, 1500, 2000])
    if parent is not None:
        lp = int(parent.size)
        n_set.add(lp)
        n_set.add(max(50, lp - 50))
        n_set.add(min(3000, lp + 50))
    n_values = []
    for n in n_set:
        n = int(n)
        if 50 <= n <= 3000:
            n_values.append(n)
    n_values.sort()

    init_info = {}
    quick_order = []
    for n in n_values:
        inits = make_inits(n, seed + 101 * n, parent=parent, k=8)
        init_info[n] = inits
        if inits:
            sc, seq = inits[0]
            quick_order.append((sc, n))
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = seq.copy()
            if n not in n_best or sc < n_best[n][0]:
                n_best[n] = (sc, seq.copy())

    quick_order.sort(key=itemgetter(0, 1))

    screen_ns = []
    if parent is not None:
        pns = int(parent.size)
        if pns in n_values:
            screen_ns.append(pns)
    for _, n in quick_order:
        if n not in screen_ns:
            screen_ns.append(n)
        if len(screen_ns) >= 6:
            break

    # Phase 1: quick screening on promising lengths.
    screen_total = min(220.0, max(50.0, max(0.0, deadline - start) * 0.20))
    if screen_ns:
        per_screen = screen_total / float(len(screen_ns))
    else:
        per_screen = 0.0

    for n in screen_ns:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 15.0:
            break
        inits = init_info.get(n)
        if not inits:
            continue
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        alloc = min(per_screen, rem * 0.35)
        if alloc < 4.0:
            continue
        a, sc = local_subgrad(
            inits[0][1],
            alloc,
            seed=seed + 7919 * n + 1,
            tau0=16.0,
            tau1=0.25,
            step0=0.55,
            max_step=2.5,
            top_frac=0.25,
            verbose=False
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
        if n not in n_best or sc < n_best[n][0]:
            n_best[n] = (sc, a.copy())

    # Phase 2: deeper polish on champion lengths.
    now = time.monotonic()
    full_budget = max(0.0, deadline - now)

    champ_list = []
    for n in screen_ns:
        if n in n_best:
            sc, seq = n_best[n]
        elif n in init_info and init_info[n]:
            sc, seq = init_info[n][0]
        else:
            continue
        champ_list.append((sc, n, seq))

    if best_seq is not None:
        nb = int(best_seq.size)
        seen_nb = False
        for item in champ_list:
            if item[1] == nb:
                seen_nb = True
                break
        if not seen_nb:
            champ_list.append((best_score, nb, best_seq.copy()))

    champ_list.sort(key=itemgetter(0, 1))

    if len(champ_list) == 1:
        fracs = [1.0]
    elif len(champ_list) == 2:
        fracs = [0.62, 0.38]
    else:
        fracs = [0.52, 0.32, 0.16]

    for idx, item in enumerate(champ_list[:3]):
        sc0, n, seq = item
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 25.0:
            break
        alloc = min(full_budget * fracs[idx], max(0.0, deadline - now))
        if alloc < 20.0:
            break

        a, sc = local_subgrad(
            seq,
            alloc,
            seed=seed + 12345 * n + idx * 1000,
            tau0=5.0,
            tau1=0.035,
            step0=0.85,
            max_step=3.0,
            top_frac=0.38,
            verbose=True
        )
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
        if n not in n_best or sc < n_best[n][0]:
            n_best[n] = (sc, a.copy())

        # Second initial for same length if time remains.
        inits = init_info.get(n)
        now = time.monotonic()
        if inits and len(inits) > 1 and now < deadline - 30.0:
            alloc2 = min(70.0, max(0.0, deadline - now) * 0.22)
            if alloc2 > 8.0:
                a2, sc2 = local_subgrad(
                    inits[1][1],
                    alloc2,
                    seed=seed + 54321 * n + idx * 997,
                    tau0=3.0,
                    tau1=0.018,
                    step0=0.75,
                    max_step=2.5,
                    top_frac=0.5,
                    verbose=False
                )
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_score:
                    best_score = sc2
                    best_seq = a2.copy()
                if n not in n_best or sc2 < n_best[n][0]:
                    n_best[n] = (sc2, a2.copy())

    # Phase 3: LP polish on moderate-size strong candidates.
    lp_candidates = []
    for sc, n, seq in champ_list:
        if n <= 700:
            if n in n_best:
                lp_candidates.append((n_best[n][0], n, n_best[n][1]))
            else:
                lp_candidates.append((sc, n, seq))
    if best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 700:
        nb = int(best_seq.size)
        seen = False
        for item in lp_candidates:
            if item[1] == nb:
                seen = True
                break
        if not seen:
            lp_candidates.append((best_score, nb, best_seq.copy()))

    lp_candidates.sort(key=itemgetter(0, 1))
    for idx, item in enumerate(lp_candidates[:2]):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        _, n, seq = item
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        if idx == 0:
            lp_budget = min(180.0, rem * 0.40)
        else:
            lp_budget = min(90.0, rem * 0.25)
        if lp_budget < 10.0:
            break
        a, sc = lp_polish(seq, lp_budget, seed=seed + 31337 * n + idx * 123)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()
        if n not in n_best or sc < n_best[n][0]:
            n_best[n] = (sc, a.copy())

    # Phase 4: final polished local search on global best.
    if best_seq is not None:
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        if rem > 35.0:
            a, sc = local_subgrad(
                best_seq,
                rem - 8.0,
                seed=seed + 999999,
                tau0=1.8,
                tau1=0.008,
                step0=1.0,
                max_step=2.6,
                top_frac=0.55,
                verbose=True
            )
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()

        # Optional final small LP if length is moderate.
        now = time.monotonic()
        rem = max(0.0, deadline - now)
        if rem > 25.0 and best_seq.size <= 500:
            a, sc = lp_polish(best_seq, rem - 5.0, seed=seed + 777777, max_n=500)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_seq = a.copy()

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.full(250, 1.0 / 250.0, dtype=np.float64)
        best_score = score_seq(best_seq)

    best_seq = normalize_seq(best_seq)
    result = [float(x) for x in np.clip(best_seq, 0.0, 1000.0)]

    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(result)
        if np.isfinite(val):
            print(f"final internal score={best_score:.8f}, validated={val:.8f}, n={len(result)}")
    except Exception:
        print(f"final internal score={best_score:.8f}, n={len(result)}")

    return result
```
